        Idea:
Build a turn-based timeline editing agent: DB session table with optimistic locking, extract shared transcription module, ai-timeline-agent edge function with tool-use loop (view/move/trim/delete/add clips, create tasks, message user, kill switch), client chat UI with voice+text input and auto-continuation, wired into VideoEditorShell

User notes and answers:
- FULL CONTEXT FROM CONVERSATION:

## Architecture decisions already made:
1. Edge function approach (not Python server) - stateless, re-invocable, DB as continuity
2. Session turns stored in DB - each invocation loads history, takes turns, writes back
3. Status field controls client behavior: done, waiting_user, continue (timeout handover)
4. Kill switch: session status set to 'cancelled', agent checks before each LLM turn
5. Optimistic locking via config_version on timelines table
6. Multiple concurrent sessions are fine - independent rows, locking handles write conflicts
7. Groq for fast LLM calls (already set up), with option for Claude on complex reasoning
8. Voice capture stays in browser, transcription extracted to shared module

## Key files:
- supabase/functions/ai-voice-prompt/index.ts - has Groq Whisper transcription to extract
- supabase/functions/_shared/edgeHandler.ts - bootstrap pattern for edge functions
- src/shared/hooks/useVoiceRecording.ts - voice capture hook
- src/tools/video-editor/types/index.ts - TimelineConfig, TimelineClip, TrackDefinition types
- src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useTimelineRealtime.ts - realtime refresh on timeline changes
- /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/announcement-video/tools/place.py - clip placement & conflict resolution
- /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/announcement-video/tools/view.py - timeline enrichment & visualization
- /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/announcement-video/tools/common.py - shared utilities

## Tool definitions to implement (ported from Python):
- view_timeline: enriched read-only view (from view.py render_view)
- move_clip(clipId, at): set clip.at (from place.py)
- trim_clip(clipId, from?, to?, duration?): adjust source range
- set_clip_property(clipId, property, value): generic setter
- delete_clip(clipId): remove from clips array
- add_text_clip(track, at, duration, text): create text overlay
- find_issues(): detect gaps, overlaps, mid-sentence cuts (from view.py find_issues)
- create_generation_task(prompt, count): queue via existing task system
- message_user(text): conversational response, sets status to waiting_user
- done(summary): terminate session

## Performance targets:
- Simple edits: <3s total (Groq LLM ~1s)
- Complex reasoning: <8s (Claude Sonnet)
- Parallel transcription + context loading

## Client requirements:
- Chat UI showing turns (messages, tool calls, results)
- Voice input via useVoiceRecording
- Text input fallback
- Auto-re-invoke on status=continue
- Kill switch button
- Session list for multiple conversations
- Realtime subscription for turn updates
- Answers to planner questions: Q1: Implement tools from the TypeScript type definitions - the Python tools are reference implementations but the logic is straightforward JSON manipulation. Q2: Floating drawer/panel on the right side, collapsible - similar to a chat sidebar. Don't replace properties panel. Q3: Yes include create_generation_task but as a simple pass-through to existing createTask - it just queues the task, doesn't need complex logic. Include it in v1. Q4: Auto-re-invoke immediately with a small delay (~300ms). Show 'Agent is thinking...' spinner but don't require user intervention - the continuation should be seamless.

  "meta_commentary": "Execution guidance:\n\n**Parallelism**: T1, T3, T4, T5, T11 have no dependencies and can all be executed in parallel as the first batch. T2 depends on T1. T6 depends on T3+T4+T5. T7 and T8 depend only on T4 so can start as soon as types exist. T9 is the critical path \u2014 it depends on T6+T7+T8.\n\n**Critical path**: T4 \u2192 T7/T8 \u2192 T6 \u2192 T9 \u2192 T14 \u2192 T15. The client side (T10\u2192T12\u2192T13) can proceed in parallel with edge function work once T3+T4 are done.\n\n**Biggest risk**: Groq tool-use support. The plan assumes Groq chat completions support function calling. If the specific model doesn't support it, you'll need to either: (a) use a Groq model that does (llama-3.3-70b-versatile supports tools), or (b) implement manual tool parsing from structured output. Verify this early in T9.\n\n**Claude model routing**: The plan mentions model='claude' for complex reasoning, but there's no Anthropic SDK in the edge function layer. For v1, use Groq only. The model field on sessions is there for future use.\n\n**Config immutability in tools**: Every tool in T7 must clone config before mutating. The loop in T9 may call multiple tools per iteration \u2014 each operates on the result of the previous. Deep-clone once at loop start, apply tools sequentially, save once at end of iteration.\n\n**Session turns storage**: Turns accumulate as JSONB array. For v1 this is fine but will eventually need pagination or archival if sessions grow past ~100 turns. Not a v1 concern.\n\n**Voice flow**: Browser records \u2192 useVoiceRecording sends to ai-voice-prompt \u2192 transcription returned \u2192 useAgentVoice passes text to useSendMessage \u2192 text sent to ai-timeline-agent. No audio goes to the timeline agent directly.\n\n**Conflict UX decision**: The plan originally proposed silently reloading on agent writes. This is explicitly rejected per FLAG remote-reload-data-loss. The existing conflict dialog behavior is preserved. When the user has no dirty edits and the agent writes, auto-refresh handles it via the existing realtime path. When the user has dirty edits, the conflict dialog appears. This is the safest approach for v1."
}

- [DEBT] realtime-subscriptions: the shared realtime system doesn't know about timeline_agent_sessions; step 7 hooks need their own channel subscription. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] task-deduplication: server-generated idempotency keys would break retry deduplication because retried requests get fresh keys. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] ta[REDACTED_SK]: create_generation_task is underspecified against the actual task family system. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

> DEVELOPER

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app

        Idea:
Build a turn-based timeline editing agent: DB session table with optimistic locking, extract shared transcription module, ai-timeline-agent edge function with tool-use loop (view/move/trim/delete/add clips, create tasks, message user, kill switch), client chat UI with voice+text input and auto-continuation, wired into VideoEditorShell

User notes and answers:
- FULL CONTEXT FROM CONVERSATION:

## Architecture decisions already made:
1. Edge function approach (not Python server) - stateless, re-invocable, DB as continuity
2. Session turns stored in DB - each invocation loads history, takes turns, writes back
3. Status field controls client behavior: done, waiting_user, continue (timeout handover)
4. Kill switch: session status set to 'cancelled', agent checks before each LLM turn
5. Optimistic locking via config_version on timelines table
6. Multiple concurrent sessions are fine - independent rows, locking handles write conflicts
7. Groq for fast LLM calls (already set up), with option for Claude on complex reasoning
8. Voice capture stays in browser, transcription extracted to shared module

## Key files:
- supabase/functions/ai-voice-prompt/index.ts - has Groq Whisper transcription to extract
- supabase/functions/_shared/edgeHandler.ts - bootstrap pattern for edge functions
- src/shared/hooks/useVoiceRecording.ts - voice capture hook
- src/tools/video-editor/types/index.ts - TimelineConfig, TimelineClip, TrackDefinition types
- src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useTimelineRealtime.ts - realtime refresh on timeline changes
- /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/announcement-video/tools/place.py - clip placement & conflict resolution
- /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/announcement-video/tools/view.py - timeline enrichment & visualization
- /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/announcement-video/tools/common.py - shared utilities

## Tool definitions to implement (ported from Python):
- view_timeline: enriched read-only view (from view.py render_view)
- move_clip(clipId, at): set clip.at (from place.py)
- trim_clip(clipId, from?, to?, duration?): adjust source range
- set_clip_property(clipId, property, value): generic setter
- delete_clip(clipId): remove from clips array
- add_text_clip(track, at, duration, text): create text overlay
- find_issues(): detect gaps, overlaps, mid-sentence cuts (from view.py find_issues)
- create_generation_task(prompt, count): queue via existing task system
- message_user(text): conversational response, sets status to waiting_user
- done(summary): terminate session

## Performance targets:
- Simple edits: <3s total (Groq LLM ~1s)
- Complex reasoning: <8s (Claude Sonnet)
- Parallel transcription + context loading

## Client requirements:
- Chat UI showing turns (messages, tool calls, results)
- Voice input via useVoiceRecording
- Text input fallback
- Auto-re-invoke on status=continue
- Kill switch button
- Session list for multiple conversations
- Realtime subscription for turn updates
- Answers to planner questions: Q1: Implement tools from the TypeScript type definitions - the Python tools are reference implementations but the logic is straightforward JSON manipulation. Q2: Floating drawer/panel on the right side, collapsible - similar to a chat sidebar. Don't replace properties panel. Q3: Yes include create_generation_task but as a simple pass-through to existing createTask - it just queues the task, doesn't need complex logic. Include it in v1. Q4: Auto-re-invoke immediately with a small delay (~300ms). Show 'Agent is thinking...' spinner but don't require user intervention - the continuation should be seamless.

On UPDATE event, invalidate the query. Clean up subscription on unmount. FLAG session-realtime-plumbing: use direct `supabase.channel()` subscription, not the shared realtime system. (3) `useCreateSession(timelineId)`: mutation to INSERT new session row. (4) `useSendMessage(sessionId)`: mutation that POSTs to ai-timeline-agent edge function via `supabase.functions.invoke('ai-timeline-agent', { body: { session_id, user_message } })`. On success, if response status === 'continue', setTimeout 300ms then re-invoke. Circuit breaker: max 10 consecutive continue re-invocations, then stop and show status. (5) `useCancelSession(sessionId)`: direct Supabase update setting status='cancelled'.",

  "meta_commentary": "Execution guidance:\n\n**Parallelism**: T1, T3, T4, T5, T11 have no dependencies and can all be executed in parallel as the first batch. T2 depends on T1. T6 depends on T3+T4+T5. T7 and T8 depend only on T4 so can start as soon as types exist. T9 is the critical path \u2014 it depends on T6+T7+T8.\n\n**Critical path**: T4 \u2192 T7/T8 \u2192 T6 \u2192 T9 \u2192 T14 \u2192 T15. The client side (T10\u2192T12\u2192T13) can proceed in parallel with edge function work once T3+T4 are done.\n\n**Biggest risk**: Groq tool-use support. The plan assumes Groq chat completions support function calling. If the specific model doesn't support it, you'll need to either: (a) use a Groq model that does (llama-3.3-70b-versatile supports tools), or (b) implement manual tool parsing from structured output. Verify this early in T9.\n\n**Claude model routing**: The plan mentions model='claude' for complex reasoning, but there's no Anthropic SDK in the edge function layer. For v1, use Groq only. The model field on sessions is there for future use.\n\n**Config immutability in tools**: Every tool in T7 must clone config before mutating. The loop in T9 may call multiple tools per iteration \u2014 each operates on the result of the previous. Deep-clone once at loop start, apply tools sequentially, save once at end of iteration.\n\n**Session turns storage**: Turns accumulate as JSONB array. For v1 this is fine but will eventually need pagination or archival if sessions grow past ~100 turns. Not a v1 concern.\n\n**Voice flow**: Browser records \u2192 useVoiceRecording sends to ai-voice-prompt \u2192 transcription returned \u2192 useAgentVoice passes text to useSendMessage \u2192 text sent to ai-timeline-agent. No audio goes to the timeline agent directly.\n\n**Conflict UX decision**: The plan originally proposed silently reloading on agent writes. This is explicitly rejected per FLAG remote-reload-data-loss. The existing conflict dialog behavior is preserved. When the user has no dirty edits and the agent writes, auto-refresh handles it via the existing realtime path. When the user has dirty edits, the conflict dialog appears. This is the safest approach for v1."
}

        Debt watch items (do not make these worse):
- [DEBT] conflict-resolution: silently reloading agent-originated timeline updates would discard dirty local edits. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] issue-detection: mid-sentence-cut detection is not implementable without transcript/profile data that the current provider doesn't expose. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] optimistic-locking: plan only specifies version checking at the db write layer; the ui load/state/save path doesn't carry config_version yet. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] realtime-subscriptions: the shared realtime system doesn't know about timeline_agent_sessions; step 7 hooks need their own channel subscription. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] task-deduplication: server-generated idempotency keys would break retry deduplication because retried requests get fresh keys. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] ta[REDACTED_SK]: create_generation_task is underspecified against the actual task family system. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

     void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ [REDACTED](timelineId) });\n+    },\n+  });\n+}\n+\n+export function useSendMessage(sessionId: string | null | undefined) {\n+  const queryClient = useQueryClient();\n+  const timeoutIdsRef = useRef<Set<number>>(new Set());\n+  const [continuationNotice, setContinuationNotice] = useState<string | null>(null);\n+\n+  useEffect(() => {\n+    return () => {\n+      for (const timeoutId of timeoutIdsRef.current) {\n+        window.clearTimeout(timeoutId);\n+      }\n+      timeoutIdsRef.current.clear();\n+    };\n+  }, []);\n+\n+  return {\n+    continuationNotice,\n+    clearContinuationNotice: () => setContinuationNotice(null),\n+    ...useMutation({\n+      mutationFn: async (userMessage: string) => {\n+        if (!sessionId) {\n+          throw new Error('sessionId is required');\n+        }\n+\n+        const invokeAgent = async (\n+          nextUserMessage?: string,\n+          continueCount = 0,\n+        ): Promise<AgentInvocationResponse> => {\n+          const { data, error } = await getSupabaseClient().functions.invoke('ai-timeline-agent', {\n+            body: {\n+              session_id: sessionId,\n+              ...(nextUserMessage ? { user_message: nextUserMessage } : {}),\n+            },\n+          });\n+\n+          if (error) {\n+            throw error;\n+          }\n+\n+          const response = normalizeInvokeResponse(data);\n+\n+          if (response.status !== 'continue') {\n+            setContinuationNotice(null);\n+            return response;\n+          }\n+\n+          if (continueCount >= AUTO_CONTINUE_LIMIT) {\n+            setContinuationNotice(\n+              `Auto-continuation paused after ${AUTO_CONTINUE_LIMIT} consecutive continue responses.`,\n+            );\n+            return response;\n+          }\n+\n+          setContinuationNotice('Agent is continuing…');\n+          await delayWithTracking(timeoutIdsRef, AUTO_CONTINUE_DELAY_MS);\n+          return invokeAgent(undefined, continueCount + 1);\n+        };\n+\n+        return invokeAgent(userMessage, 0);\n+      },\n+      onSuccess: () => {\n+        void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ [REDACTED](sessionId) });\n+        void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['timeline-agent-sessions'] });\n+      },\n+    }),\n+  };\n+}\n+\n+export function useCancelSession(sessionId: string | null | undefined) {\n+  const queryClient = useQueryClient();\n+\n+  return useMutation({\n+    mutationFn: async () => {\n+      if (!sessionId) {\n+        throw new Error('sessionId is required');\n+      }\n+\n+      const { error } = await getSupabaseClient()\n+        .from(TIMELINE_AGENT_SESSIONS_TABLE)\n+        .update({\n+          status: 'cancelled',\n+          updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),\n+        })\n+        .eq('id', sessionId);\n+\n+      if (error) {\n+        throw error;\n+      }\n+    },\n+    onSuccess: () => {\n+      void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ [REDACTED](sessionId) });\n+      void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['timeline-agent-sessions'] });\n+    },\n+  });\n+}\n*** End Patch\n"

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app

        Idea:
Build a turn-based timeline editing agent: DB session table with optimistic locking, extract shared transcription module, ai-timeline-agent edge function with tool-use loop (view/move/trim/delete/add clips, create tasks, message user, kill switch), client chat UI with voice+text input and auto-continuation, wired into VideoEditorShell

User notes and answers:
- FULL CONTEXT FROM CONVERSATION:

## Architecture decisions already made:
1. Edge function approach (not Python server) - stateless, re-invocable, DB as continuity
2. Session turns stored in DB - each invocation loads history, takes turns, writes back
3. Status field controls client behavior: done, waiting_user, continue (timeout handover)
4. Kill switch: session status set to 'cancelled', agent checks before each LLM turn
5. Optimistic locking via config_version on timelines table
6. Multiple concurrent sessions are fine - independent rows, locking handles write conflicts
7. Groq for fast LLM calls (already set up), with option for Claude on complex reasoning
8. Voice capture stays in browser, transcription extracted to shared module

## Key files:
- supabase/functions/ai-voice-prompt/index.ts - has Groq Whisper transcription to extract
- supabase/functions/_shared/edgeHandler.ts - bootstrap pattern for edge functions
- src/shared/hooks/useVoiceRecording.ts - voice capture hook
- src/tools/video-editor/types/index.ts - TimelineConfig, TimelineClip, TrackDefinition types
- src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useTimelineRealtime.ts - realtime refresh on timeline changes
- /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/announcement-video/tools/place.py - clip placement & conflict resolution
- /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/announcement-video/tools/view.py - timeline enrichment & visualization
- /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/announcement-video/tools/common.py - shared utilities

## Tool definitions to implement (ported from Python):
- view_timeline: enriched read-only view (from view.py render_view)
- move_clip(clipId, at): set clip.at (from place.py)
- trim_clip(clipId, from?, to?, duration?): adjust source range
- set_clip_property(clipId, property, value): generic setter
- delete_clip(clipId): remove from clips array
- add_text_clip(track, at, duration, text): create text overlay
- find_issues(): detect gaps, overlaps, mid-sentence cuts (from view.py find_issues)
- create_generation_task(prompt, count): queue via existing task system
- message_user(text): conversational response, sets status to waiting_user
- done(summary): terminate session

## Performance targets:
- Simple edits: <3s total (Groq LLM ~1s)
- Complex reasoning: <8s (Claude Sonnet)
- Parallel transcription + context loading

## Client requirements:
- Chat UI showing turns (messages, tool calls, results)
- Voice input via useVoiceRecording
- Text input fallback
- Auto-re-invoke on status=continue
- Kill switch button
- Session list for multiple conversations
- Realtime subscription for turn updates
- Answers to planner questions: Q1: Implement tools from the TypeScript type definitions - the Python tools are reference implementations but the logic is straightforward JSON manipulation. Q2: Floating drawer/panel on the right side, collapsible - similar to a chat sidebar. Don't replace properties panel. Q3: Yes include create_generation_task but as a simple pass-through to existing createTask - it just queues the task, doesn't need complex logic. Include it in v1. Q4: Auto-re-invoke immediately with a small delay (~300ms). Show 'Agent is thinking...' spinner but don't require user intervention - the continuation should be seamless.

      "id": "T7",
      "description": "Implement timeline manipulation tools in `supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/timeline-tools.ts`. Each tool takes (config: TimelineConfig, assetRegistry, args) and returns { result: string, config?: TimelineConfig } (config present only if mutated). Tools: (1) `view_timeline`: list tracks, clips with id/at/duration/track/type/asset, total duration. (2) `move_clip(clipId, at)`: find clip, update .at, return old/new. (3) `trim_clip(clipId, {from?, to?, duration?})`: adjust source range, validate against asset duration from registry. (4) `set_clip_property(clipId, property, value)`: whitelist: volume, speed, opacity, x, y, width, height. (5) `delete_clip(clipId)`: remove from clips array, return deleted info. (6) `add_text_clip(track, at, duration, text)`: create new clip with clipType:'text', generate UUID. (7) `find_issues(config, registry)`: detect gaps on same track, overlapping clips, clips exceeding asset duration. NOTE on FLAG find-issues-transcript-gap: mid-sentence-cut detection is NOT implementable without transcript data \u2014 implement gaps/overlaps/duration only, add a comment noting the limitation.",
      "depends_on": [
        "T4"
      ],
      "status": "done",
      "executor_notes": "Implemented the pure timeline manipulation helpers in `timeline-tools.ts`.       "id": "T9",
      "description": "Implement the tool-use loop in `ai-timeline-agent/index.ts`. After user message appended: (1) Load `timelines.config` + `config_version` via admin client. (2) Build messages array from session turns. (3) System prompt with tool descriptions + current timeline state (call view_timeline). (4) Loop max 8 iterations: call Groq `groq.chat.completions.create()` with tools \u2192 if text-only response, call message_user, break \u2192 if tool_calls, execute each, append tool_call + tool_result turns \u2192 if config mutated, save via `update_timeline_config_versioned` RPC, on version conflict reload config and retry tool once \u2192 if stopLoop signal (message_user/done), break \u2192 re-read session status from DB, if 'cancelled' break. (5) Timeout safety: track Date.now(), if approaching 50s, set status='continue', save, return. (6) If loop hits 8 iterations without terminal tool, set status='continue'. (7) Final: save all accumulated turns to session, return { status, turns_added }. Create `supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/prompts.ts` with system prompt template.",

  "meta_commentary": "Execution guidance:\n\n**Parallelism**: T1, T3, T4, T5, T11 have no dependencies and can all be executed in parallel as the first batch. T2 depends on T1. T6 depends on T3+T4+T5. T7 and T8 depend only on T4 so can start as soon as types exist. T9 is the critical path \u2014 it depends on T6+T7+T8.\n\n**Critical path**: T4 \u2192 T7/T8 \u2192 T6 \u2192 T9 \u2192 T14 \u2192 T15. The client side (T10\u2192T12\u2192T13) can proceed in parallel with edge function work once T3+T4 are done.\n\n**Biggest risk**: Groq tool-use support. The plan assumes Groq chat completions support function calling. If the specific model doesn't support it, you'll need to either: (a) use a Groq model that does (llama-3.3-70b-versatile supports tools), or (b) implement manual tool parsing from structured output. Verify this early in T9.\n\n**Claude model routing**: The plan mentions model='claude' for complex reasoning, but there's no Anthropic SDK in the edge function layer. For v1, use Groq only. The model field on sessions is there for future use.\n\n**Config immutability in tools**: Every tool in T7 must clone config before mutating. The loop in T9 may call multiple tools per iteration \u2014 each operates on the result of the previous. Deep-clone once at loop start, apply tools sequentially, save once at end of iteration.\n\n**Session turns storage**: Turns accumulate as JSONB array. For v1 this is fine but will eventually need pagination or archival if sessions grow past ~100 turns. Not a v1 concern.\n\n**Voice flow**: Browser records \u2192 useVoiceRecording sends to ai-voice-prompt \u2192 transcription returned \u2192 useAgentVoice passes text to useSendMessage \u2192 text sent to ai-timeline-agent. No audio goes to the timeline agent directly.\n\n**Conflict UX decision**: The plan originally proposed silently reloading on agent writes. This is explicitly rejected per FLAG remote-reload-data-loss. The existing conflict dialog behavior is preserved. When the user has no dirty edits and the agent writes, auto-refresh handles it via the existing realtime path. When the user has dirty edits, the conflict dialog appears. This is the safest approach for v1."
}

        Debt watch items (do not make these worse):
- [DEBT] conflict-resolution: silently reloading agent-originated timeline updates would discard dirty local edits. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] issue-detection: mid-sentence-cut detection is not implementable without transcript/profile data that the current provider doesn't expose. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] optimistic-locking: plan only specifies version checking at the db write layer; the ui load/state/save path doesn't carry config_version yet. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] realtime-subscriptions: the shared realtime system doesn't know about timeline_agent_sessions; step 7 hooks need their own channel subscription. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] task-deduplication: server-generated idempotency keys would break retry deduplication because retried requests get fresh keys. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] ta[REDACTED_SK]: create_generation_task is underspecified against the actual task family system. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

        Note: User explicitly approved this plan at the gate checkpoint.
        Robustness level: standard.

        - Do not include updates for tasks or sense checks outside this batch.
        - Keep `executor_notes` verification-focused.

> DEVELOPER

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app

        Idea:
Build a turn-based timeline editing agent: DB session table with optimistic locking, extract shared transcription module, ai-timeline-agent edge function with tool-use loop (view/move/trim/delete/add clips, create tasks, message user, kill switch), client chat UI with voice+text input and auto-continuation, wired into VideoEditorShell

User notes and answers:
- FULL CONTEXT FROM CONVERSATION:

## Architecture decisions already made:
1. Edge function approach (not Python server) - stateless, re-invocable, DB as continuity
2. Session turns stored in DB - each invocation loads history, takes turns, writes back
3. Status field controls client behavior: done, waiting_user, continue (timeout handover)
4. Kill switch: session status set to 'cancelled', agent checks before each LLM turn
5. Optimistic locking via config_version on timelines table
6. Multiple concurrent sessions are fine - independent rows, locking handles write conflicts
7. Groq for fast LLM calls (already set up), with option for Claude on complex reasoning
8. Voice capture stays in browser, transcription extracted to shared module

## Key files:
- supabase/functions/ai-voice-prompt/index.ts - has Groq Whisper transcription to extract
- supabase/functions/_shared/edgeHandler.ts - bootstrap pattern for edge functions
- src/shared/hooks/useVoiceRecording.ts - voice capture hook
- src/tools/video-editor/types/index.ts - TimelineConfig, TimelineClip, TrackDefinition types
- src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useTimelineRealtime.ts - realtime refresh on timeline changes
- /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/announcement-video/tools/place.py - clip placement & conflict resolution
- /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/announcement-video/tools/view.py - timeline enrichment & visualization
- /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/announcement-video/tools/common.py - shared utilities

## Tool definitions to implement (ported from Python):
- view_timeline: enriched read-only view (from view.py render_view)
- move_clip(clipId, at): set clip.at (from place.py)
- trim_clip(clipId, from?, to?, duration?): adjust source range
- set_clip_property(clipId, property, value): generic setter
- delete_clip(clipId): remove from clips array
- add_text_clip(track, at, duration, text): create text overlay
- find_issues(): detect gaps, overlaps, mid-sentence cuts (from view.py find_issues)
- create_generation_task(prompt, count): queue via existing task system
- message_user(text): conversational response, sets status to waiting_user
- done(summary): terminate session

## Performance targets:
- Simple edits: <3s total (Groq LLM ~1s)
- Complex reasoning: <8s (Claude Sonnet)
- Parallel transcription + context loading

## Client requirements:
- Chat UI showing turns (messages, tool calls, results)
- Voice input via useVoiceRecording
- Text input fallback
- Auto-re-invoke on status=continue
- Kill switch button
- Session list for multiple conversations
- Realtime subscription for turn updates
- Answers to planner questions: Q1: Implement tools from the TypeScript type definitions - the Python tools are reference implementations but the logic is straightforward JSON manipulation. Q2: Floating drawer/panel on the right side, collapsible - similar to a chat sidebar. Don't replace properties panel. Q3: Yes include create_generation_task but as a simple pass-through to existing createTask - it just queues the task, doesn't need complex logic. Include it in v1. Q4: Auto-re-invoke immediately with a small delay (~300ms). Show 'Agent is thinking...' spinner but don't require user intervention - the continuation should be seamless.

(5) Timeout safety: track Date.now(), if approaching 50s, set status='continue', save, return. (6) If loop hits 8 iterations without terminal tool, set status='continue'.       "id": "T9",
      "description": "Implement the tool-use loop in `ai-timeline-agent/index.ts`. After user message appended: (1) Load `timelines.config` + `config_version` via admin client. (2) Build messages array from session turns. (3) System prompt with tool descriptions + current timeline state (call view_timeline). (4) Loop max 8 iterations: call Groq `groq.chat.completions.create()` with tools \u2192 if text-only response, call message_user, break \u2192 if tool_calls, execute each, append tool_call + tool_result turns \u2192 if config mutated, save via `update_timeline_config_versioned` RPC, on version conflict reload config and retry tool once \u2192 if stopLoop signal (message_user/done), break \u2192 re-read session status from DB, if 'cancelled' break. (5) Timeout safety: track Date.now(), if approaching 50s, set status='continue', save, return. (6) If loop hits 8 iterations without terminal tool, set status='continue'. (7) Final: save all accumulated turns to session, return { status, turns_added }. Create `supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/prompts.ts` with system prompt template.",

  "meta_commentary": "Execution guidance:\n\n**Parallelism**: T1, T3, T4, T5, T11 have no dependencies and can all be executed in parallel as the first batch. T2 depends on T1. T6 depends on T3+T4+T5. T7 and T8 depend only on T4 so can start as soon as types exist. T9 is the critical path \u2014 it depends on T6+T7+T8.\n\n**Critical path**: T4 \u2192 T7/T8 \u2192 T6 \u2192 T9 \u2192 T14 \u2192 T15. The client side (T10\u2192T12\u2192T13) can proceed in parallel with edge function work once T3+T4 are done.\n\n**Biggest risk**: Groq tool-use support. The plan assumes Groq chat completions support function calling. If the specific model doesn't support it, you'll need to either: (a) use a Groq model that does (llama-3.3-70b-versatile supports tools), or (b) implement manual tool parsing from structured output. Verify this early in T9.\n\n**Claude model routing**: The plan mentions model='claude' for complex reasoning, but there's no Anthropic SDK in the edge function layer. For v1, use Groq only. The model field on sessions is there for future use.\n\n**Config immutability in tools**: Every tool in T7 must clone config before mutating. The loop in T9 may call multiple tools per iteration \u2014 each operates on the result of the previous. Deep-clone once at loop start, apply tools sequentially, save once at end of iteration.\n\n**Session turns storage**: Turns accumulate as JSONB array. For v1 this is fine but will eventually need pagination or archival if sessions grow past ~100 turns. Not a v1 concern.\n\n**Voice flow**: Browser records \u2192 useVoiceRecording sends to ai-voice-prompt \u2192 transcription returned \u2192 useAgentVoice passes text to useSendMessage \u2192 text sent to ai-timeline-agent. No audio goes to the timeline agent directly.\n\n**Conflict UX decision**: The plan originally proposed silently reloading on agent writes. This is explicitly rejected per FLAG remote-reload-data-loss. The existing conflict dialog behavior is preserved. When the user has no dirty edits and the agent writes, auto-refresh handles it via the existing realtime path. When the user has dirty edits, the conflict dialog appears. This is the safest approach for v1."
}

> AGENT

{"output":"Completed batch 4 tasks T13 and T14. > DEVELOPER

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app

        Idea:
Build a turn-based timeline editing agent: DB session table with optimistic locking, extract shared transcription module, ai-timeline-agent edge function with tool-use loop (view/move/trim/delete/add clips, create tasks, message user, kill switch), client chat UI with voice+text input and auto-continuation, wired into VideoEditorShell

User notes and answers:
- FULL CONTEXT FROM CONVERSATION:

## Architecture decisions already made:
1. Edge function approach (not Python server) - stateless, re-invocable, DB as continuity
2. Session turns stored in DB - each invocation loads history, takes turns, writes back
3. Status field controls client behavior: done, waiting_user, continue (timeout handover)
4. Kill switch: session status set to 'cancelled', agent checks before each LLM turn
5. Optimistic locking via config_version on timelines table
6. Multiple concurrent sessions are fine - independent rows, locking handles write conflicts
7. Groq for fast LLM calls (already set up), with option for Claude on complex reasoning
8. Voice capture stays in browser, transcription extracted to shared module

## Key files:
- supabase/functions/ai-voice-prompt/index.ts - has Groq Whisper transcription to extract
- supabase/functions/_shared/edgeHandler.ts - bootstrap pattern for edge functions
- src/shared/hooks/useVoiceRecording.ts - voice capture hook
- src/tools/video-editor/types/index.ts - TimelineConfig, TimelineClip, TrackDefinition types
- src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useTimelineRealtime.ts - realtime refresh on timeline changes
- /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/announcement-video/tools/place.py - clip placement & conflict resolution
- /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/announcement-video/tools/view.py - timeline enrichment & visualization
- /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/announcement-video/tools/common.py - shared utilities

## Tool definitions to implement (ported from Python):
- view_timeline: enriched read-only view (from view.py render_view)
- move_clip(clipId, at): set clip.at (from place.py)
- trim_clip(clipId, from?, to?, duration?): adjust source range
- set_clip_property(clipId, property, value): generic setter
- delete_clip(clipId): remove from clips array
- add_text_clip(track, at, duration, text): create text overlay
- find_issues(): detect gaps, overlaps, mid-sentence cuts (from view.py find_issues)
- create_generation_task(prompt, count): queue via existing task system
- message_user(text): conversational response, sets status to waiting_user
- done(summary): terminate session

## Performance targets:
- Simple edits: <3s total (Groq LLM ~1s)
- Complex reasoning: <8s (Claude Sonnet)
- Parallel transcription + context loading

## Client requirements:
- Chat UI showing turns (messages, tool calls, results)
- Voice input via useVoiceRecording
- Text input fallback
- Auto-re-invoke on status=continue
- Kill switch button
- Session list for multiple conversations
- Realtime subscription for turn updates
- Answers to planner questions: Q1: Implement tools from the TypeScript type definitions - the Python tools are reference implementations but the logic is straightforward JSON manipulation. Q2: Floating drawer/panel on the right side, collapsible - similar to a chat sidebar. Don't replace properties panel. Q3: Yes include create_generation_task but as a simple pass-through to existing createTask - it just queues the task, doesn't need complex logic. Include it in v1. Q4: Auto-re-invoke immediately with a small delay (~300ms). Show 'Agent is thinking...' spinner but don't require user intervention - the continuation should be seamless.

Tools: (1) `view_timeline`: list tracks, clips with id/at/duration/track/type/asset, total duration. (2) `move_clip(clipId, at)`: find clip, update .at, return old/new. (3) `trim_clip(clipId, {from?, to?, duration?})`: adjust source range, validate against asset duration from registry. (4) `set_clip_property(clipId, property, value)`: whitelist: volume, speed, opacity, x, y, width, height. (5) `delete_clip(clipId)`: remove from clips array, return deleted info. (6) `add_text_clip(track, at, duration, text)`: create new clip with clipType:'text', generate UUID. (7) `find_issues(config, registry)`: detect gaps on same track, overlapping clips, clips exceeding asset duration. (4) Loop max 8 iterations: call Groq `groq.chat.completions.create()` with tools \u2192 if text-only response, call message_user, break \u2192 if tool_calls, execute each, append tool_call + tool_result turns \u2192 if config mutated, save via `update_timeline_config_versioned` RPC, on version conflict reload config and retry tool once \u2192 if stopLoop signal (message_user/done), break \u2192 re-read session status from DB, if 'cancelled' break. (5) Timeout safety: track Date.now(), if approaching 50s, set status='continue', save, return. (6) If loop hits 8 iterations without terminal tool, set status='continue'.   "meta_commentary": "Execution guidance:\n\n**Parallelism**: T1, T3, T4, T5, T11 have no dependencies and can all be executed in parallel as the first batch. T2 depends on T1. T6 depends on T3+T4+T5. T7 and T8 depend only on T4 so can start as soon as types exist. T9 is the critical path \u2014 it depends on T6+T7+T8.\n\n**Critical path**: T4 \u2192 T7/T8 \u2192 T6 \u2192 T9 \u2192 T14 \u2192 T15. The client side (T10\u2192T12\u2192T13) can proceed in parallel with edge function work once T3+T4 are done.\n\n**Biggest risk**: Groq tool-use support. The plan assumes Groq chat completions support function calling. If the specific model doesn't support it, you'll need to either: (a) use a Groq model that does (llama-3.3-70b-versatile supports tools), or (b) implement manual tool parsing from structured output. Verify this early in T9.\n\n**Claude model routing**: The plan mentions model='claude' for complex reasoning, but there's no Anthropic SDK in the edge function layer. For v1, use Groq only. The model field on sessions is there for future use.\n\n**Config immutability in tools**: Every tool in T7 must clone config before mutating. The loop in T9 may call multiple tools per iteration \u2014 each operates on the result of the previous. Deep-clone once at loop start, apply tools sequentially, save once at end of iteration.\n\n**Session turns storage**: Turns accumulate as JSONB array. For v1 this is fine but will eventually need pagination or archival if sessions grow past ~100 turns. Not a v1 concern.\n\n**Voice flow**: Browser records \u2192 useVoiceRecording sends to ai-voice-prompt \u2192 transcription returned \u2192 useAgentVoice passes text to useSendMessage \u2192 text sent to ai-timeline-agent. No audio goes to the timeline agent directly.\n\n**Conflict UX decision**: The plan originally proposed silently reloading on agent writes. This is explicitly rejected per FLAG remote-reload-data-loss. The existing conflict dialog behavior is preserved. When the user has no dirty edits and the agent writes, auto-refresh handles it via the existing realtime path. When the user has dirty edits, the conflict dialog appears. This is the safest approach for v1."
}

        Debt watch items (do not make these worse):
- [DEBT] conflict-resolution: silently reloading agent-originated timeline updates would discard dirty local edits. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] issue-detection: mid-sentence-cut detection is not implementable without transcript/profile data that the current provider doesn't expose. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] optimistic-locking: plan only specifies version checking at the db write layer; the ui load/state/save path doesn't carry config_version yet. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] realtime-subscriptions: the shared realtime system doesn't know about timeline_agent_sessions; step 7 hooks need their own channel subscription. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] task-deduplication: server-generated idempotency keys would break retry deduplication because retried requests get fresh keys. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] ta[REDACTED_SK]: create_generation_task is underspecified against the actual task family system. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

        Note: User explicitly approved this plan at the gate checkpoint.
        Robustness level: standard.

        - Only produce `task_updates` for these tasks: [T15]

        - Do not include updates for tasks or sense checks outside this batch.
        - Keep `executor_notes` verification-focused.

> DEVELOPER

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app

        Idea:
Build a turn-based timeline editing agent: DB session table with optimistic locking, extract shared transcription module, ai-timeline-agent edge function with tool-use loop (view/move/trim/delete/add clips, create tasks, message user, kill switch), client chat UI with voice+text input and auto-continuation, wired into VideoEditorShell

User notes and answers:
- FULL CONTEXT FROM CONVERSATION:

## Architecture decisions already made:
1. Edge function approach (not Python server) - stateless, re-invocable, DB as continuity
2. Session turns stored in DB - each invocation loads history, takes turns, writes back
3. Status field controls client behavior: done, waiting_user, continue (timeout handover)
4. Kill switch: session status set to 'cancelled', agent checks before each LLM turn
5. Optimistic locking via config_version on timelines table
6. Multiple concurrent sessions are fine - independent rows, locking handles write conflicts
7. Groq for fast LLM calls (already set up), with option for Claude on complex reasoning
8. Voice capture stays in browser, transcription extracted to shared module

## Key files:
- supabase/functions/ai-voice-prompt/index.ts - has Groq Whisper transcription to extract
- supabase/functions/_shared/edgeHandler.ts - bootstrap pattern for edge functions
- src/shared/hooks/useVoiceRecording.ts - voice capture hook
- src/tools/video-editor/types/index.ts - TimelineConfig, TimelineClip, TrackDefinition types
- src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useTimelineRealtime.ts - realtime refresh on timeline changes
- /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/announcement-video/tools/place.py - clip placement & conflict resolution
- /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/announcement-video/tools/view.py - timeline enrichment & visualization
- /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/announcement-video/tools/common.py - shared utilities

## Tool definitions to implement (ported from Python):
- view_timeline: enriched read-only view (from view.py render_view)
- move_clip(clipId, at): set clip.at (from place.py)
- trim_clip(clipId, from?, to?, duration?): adjust source range
- set_clip_property(clipId, property, value): generic setter
- delete_clip(clipId): remove from clips array
- add_text_clip(track, at, duration, text): create text overlay
- find_issues(): detect gaps, overlaps, mid-sentence cuts (from view.py find_issues)
- create_generation_task(prompt, count): queue via existing task system
- message_user(text): conversational response, sets status to waiting_user
- done(summary): terminate session

## Performance targets:
- Simple edits: <3s total (Groq LLM ~1s)
- Complex reasoning: <8s (Claude Sonnet)
- Parallel transcription + context loading

## Client requirements:
- Chat UI showing turns (messages, tool calls, results)
- Voice input via useVoiceRecording
- Text input fallback
- Auto-re-invoke on status=continue
- Kill switch button
- Session list for multiple conversations
- Realtime subscription for turn updates
- Answers to planner questions: Q1: Implement tools from the TypeScript type definitions - the Python tools are reference implementations but the logic is straightforward JSON manipulation. Q2: Floating drawer/panel on the right side, collapsible - similar to a chat sidebar. Don't replace properties panel. Q3: Yes include create_generation_task but as a simple pass-through to existing createTask - it just queues the task, doesn't need complex logic. Include it in v1. Q4: Auto-re-invoke immediately with a small delay (~300ms). Show 'Agent is thinking...' spinner but don't require user intervention - the continuation should be seamless.

        Batch framing:

(7) `find_issues(config, registry)`: detect gaps on same track, overlapping clips, clips exceeding asset duration. (5) Timeout safety: track Date.now(), if approaching 50s, set status='continue', save, return. (6) If loop hits 8 iterations without terminal tool, set status='continue'.       "id": "T7",
      "description": "Implement timeline manipulation tools in `supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/timeline-tools.ts`. Each tool takes (config: TimelineConfig, assetRegistry, args) and returns { result: string, config?: TimelineConfig } (config present only if mutated). Tools: (1) `view_timeline`: list tracks, clips with id/at/duration/track/type/asset, total duration. (2) `move_clip(clipId, at)`: find clip, update .at, return old/new. (3) `trim_clip(clipId, {from?, to?, duration?})`: adjust source range, validate against asset duration from registry. (4) `set_clip_property(clipId, property, value)`: whitelist: volume, speed, opacity, x, y, width, height. (5) `delete_clip(clipId)`: remove from clips array, return deleted info. (6) `add_text_clip(track, at, duration, text)`: create new clip with clipType:'text', generate UUID. (7) `find_issues(config, registry)`: detect gaps on same track, overlapping clips, clips exceeding asset duration. NOTE on FLAG find-issues-transcript-gap: mid-sentence-cut detection is NOT implementable without transcript data \u2014 implement gaps/overlaps/duration only, add a comment noting the limitation.",
      "depends_on": [
        "T4"

      "executor_notes": "Implemented the pure timeline manipulation helpers in `timeline-tools.ts`.       "id": "T9",
      "description": "Implement the tool-use loop in `ai-timeline-agent/index.ts`. After user message appended: (1) Load `timelines.config` + `config_version` via admin client. (2) Build messages array from session turns. (3) System prompt with tool descriptions + current timeline state (call view_timeline). (4) Loop max 8 iterations: call Groq `groq.chat.completions.create()` with tools \u2192 if text-only response, call message_user, break \u2192 if tool_calls, execute each, append tool_call + tool_result turns \u2192 if config mutated, save via `update_timeline_config_versioned` RPC, on version conflict reload config and retry tool once \u2192 if stopLoop signal (message_user/done), break \u2192 re-read session status from DB, if 'cancelled' break. (5) Timeout safety: track Date.now(), if approaching 50s, set status='continue', save, return. (6) If loop hits 8 iterations without terminal tool, set status='continue'. (7) Final: save all accumulated turns to session, return { status, turns_added }. Create `supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/prompts.ts` with system prompt template.",

  "meta_commentary": "Execution guidance:\n\n**Parallelism**: T1, T3, T4, T5, T11 have no dependencies and can all be executed in parallel as the first batch. T2 depends on T1. T6 depends on T3+T4+T5. T7 and T8 depend only on T4 so can start as soon as types exist. T9 is the critical path \u2014 it depends on T6+T7+T8.\n\n**Critical path**: T4 \u2192 T7/T8 \u2192 T6 \u2192 T9 \u2192 T14 \u2192 T15. The client side (T10\u2192T12\u2192T13) can proceed in parallel with edge function work once T3+T4 are done.\n\n**Biggest risk**: Groq tool-use support. The plan assumes Groq chat completions support function calling. If the specific model doesn't support it, you'll need to either: (a) use a Groq model that does (llama-3.3-70b-versatile supports tools), or (b) implement manual tool parsing from structured output. Verify this early in T9.\n\n**Claude model routing**: The plan mentions model='claude' for complex reasoning, but there's no Anthropic SDK in the edge function layer. For v1, use Groq only. The model field on sessions is there for future use.\n\n**Config immutability in tools**: Every tool in T7 must clone config before mutating. The loop in T9 may call multiple tools per iteration \u2014 each operates on the result of the previous. Deep-clone once at loop start, apply tools sequentially, save once at end of iteration.\n\n**Session turns storage**: Turns accumulate as JSONB array. For v1 this is fine but will eventually need pagination or archival if sessions grow past ~100 turns. Not a v1 concern.\n\n**Voice flow**: Browser records \u2192 useVoiceRecording sends to ai-voice-prompt \u2192 transcription returned \u2192 useAgentVoice passes text to useSendMessage \u2192 text sent to ai-timeline-agent. No audio goes to the timeline agent directly.\n\n**Conflict UX decision**: The plan originally proposed silently reloading on agent writes. This is explicitly rejected per FLAG remote-reload-data-loss. The existing conflict dialog behavior is preserved. When the user has no dirty edits and the agent writes, auto-refresh handles it via the existing realtime path. When the user has dirty edits, the conflict dialog appears. This is the safest approach for v1."
}
